# DOG Language 2,040-Capability & Upgrade Catalog

Complete non-duplicate taxonomy (`DOG-0001` through `DOG-2040`) across 40 engineering & upgrade domains.

- `tested`: Implemented in `src/` and exercised by automated tests in `tests/`
- `implemented`: Built and active in `src/`
- `specified`: Syntax, type contract, and edge cases defined for upcoming milestones
- `proposed`: Reserved capability slot in the 20-domain taxonomy

## 1. Variables & Data Types (60 Capabilities: DOG-0001..DOG-0060)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0001 | Immutable let binding | `let x = value` | E012 on reassignment | tested |
| DOG-0002 | Mutable var binding | `var x = value` | Allows reassignment via = | tested |
| DOG-0003 | Constant const binding | `const NAME = value` | Exportable across use modules | tested |
| DOG-0004 | Explicit type annotation | `let x: number = 10` | E021 if initializer mismatches | tested |
| DOG-0005 | UTF-8 text primitive | `let s: text = "hi"` | Single-line escaped strings; E002 if unterminated | tested |
| DOG-0006 | Signed 64-bit integer number | `let n: number = 42` | Range i64; E003 on overflow | tested |
| DOG-0007 | IEEE 754 decimal type | `let d: decimal = 3.14` | Requires explicit cast from number | tested |
| DOG-0008 | Boolean primitive type | `let ok: bool = true` | Required by if/while conditions | tested |
| DOG-0009 | Void return unit type | `-> void` | Cannot be assigned to bindings (E021) | tested |
| DOG-0010 | Explicit text() cast | `text(expr)` | Formats any non-void value | tested |
| DOG-0011 | Explicit number() cast | `number(expr)` | E030 if text is not integer | tested |
| DOG-0012 | Explicit decimal() cast | `decimal(expr)` | E030 if text is not valid float | tested |
| DOG-0013 | Typed empty list annotation | `var a: list<text> = []` | E024 if [] has no type hint | tested |
| DOG-0014 | Typed empty map annotation | `var m: map<text, number> = {}` | E024 if {} has no type hint | tested |
| DOG-0015 | Duplicate binding guard | `Scope name uniqueness` | E011 on duplicate in same scope | tested |
| DOG-0016 | Type/binding collision guard | `No shadowing struct/enum names` | E011 on collision | implemented |
| DOG-0017 | Lexical block scoping | `if/while/for/fn block scopes` | Inner bindings do not leak | tested |
| DOG-0018 | String escape sequences | `\n \t \r \" \\` | Preserved during lexing | tested |
| DOG-0019 | UTF-8 character indexing | `txt[0] -> text` | E034 on out-of-bounds | implemented |
| DOG-0020 | Explicit no-implicit-coercion | `text + number rejected` | E021 diagnostic emitted | tested |
| DOG-0021 | String interpolation literals | `$"Hello {name}!"` | Strict expression typing; `{{`/`}}` escapes literal braces; forbids void | tested |
| DOG-0022 | String index search index_of() | `index_of(haystack, needle) -> number` | Returns byte/char index or -1 when absent | tested |
| DOG-0023 | Glob pattern matching | `glob_match(pattern, text) -> bool` | Supports * and ? wildcards cleanly | tested |
| DOG-0024 | String repeat & padding helpers | `str_repeat() / pad_left() / pad_right()` | Safe repetition & column alignment | tested |
| DOG-0025 | HTML entity escaping helper | `html_escape(s) -> text` | Escapes &, <, >, ", ' against XSS | tested |
| DOG-0026 | Clamped & trig math helpers | `clamp() / sin() / cos() / log()` | Native interpreter & Rust codegen lowering | tested |
| DOG-0027 | Variables & Data Types: Type refinement, tuple/slice primitives, and literal forms (item 7) | `roadmap::d01::cap_07` | Must uphold strict typing, zero implicit coercion, and explicit E-code diagnostics | specified |
| DOG-0028 | Variables & Data Types: Type refinement, tuple/slice primitives, and literal forms (item 8) | `roadmap::d01::cap_08` | Must uphold strict typing, zero implicit coercion, and explicit E-code diagnostics | specified |

## 2. Functions & Execution (70 Capabilities: DOG-0061..DOG-0130)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0061 | Function declaration | `fn name(p: type) -> ret ... end` | Top-level functions hoisted for recursion | tested |
| DOG-0062 | Implicit void function | `fn greet(n: text) ... end` | Rejects return <value> with E021 | tested |
| DOG-0063 | Typed parameter checking | `fn add(a: number, b: number)` | E021 on mismatched arg type | tested |
| DOG-0064 | Strict arity verification | `fn f(a: text)` | E014 on wrong arg count | tested |
| DOG-0065 | Exhaustive return path check | `All branches return` | E026 if branch misses return | tested |
| DOG-0066 | Two-pass mutual recursion | `a() calls b() declared later` | Registered before body check | tested |
| DOG-0067 | Stack call depth guard | `MAX_CALL_DEPTH = 256` | E032 prevents stack overflow | implemented |
| DOG-0068 | Built-in name reservation | `Cannot redefine say/number/etc.` | E011 diagnostic | implemented |
| DOG-0069 | Duplicate parameter guard | `fn f(x: text, x: text)` | E011 diagnostic | implemented |
| DOG-0070 | Duplicate function guard | `Two fn with same name` | E011 diagnostic | implemented |
| DOG-0071 | Native say() stdout output | `say(val)` | Accepts any non-void printable type | tested |
| DOG-0072 | Interactive answer() input | `answer(prompt?)` | Always returns text; E040 on IO failure | tested |
| DOG-0073 | Structured log_info() | `log_info(msg)` | Prefixes [INFO] cleanly | implemented |
| DOG-0074 | Structured log_warn() | `log_warn(msg)` | Prefixes [WARN] cleanly | implemented |
| DOG-0075 | Structured log_error() | `log_error(msg)` | Prefixes [ERROR] cleanly | implemented |
| DOG-0076 | Top-level sequential script execution | `Entry .dg file execution` | Stops cleanly on unhandled diagnostic | tested |

## 3. Operators & Expressions (50 Capabilities: DOG-0131..DOG-0180)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0131 | Strict equality operator | `a == b -> bool` | Operands must have identical types (E021) | tested |
| DOG-0132 | Strict inequality operator | `a != b -> bool` | Rejects cross-type comparison | tested |
| DOG-0133 | Greater than comparison | `a > b -> bool` | Numeric matching types only | tested |
| DOG-0134 | Less than comparison | `a < b -> bool` | Numeric matching types only | tested |
| DOG-0135 | Greater or equal comparison | `a >= b -> bool` | Numeric matching types only | tested |
| DOG-0136 | Less or equal comparison | `a <= b -> bool` | Numeric matching types only | tested |
| DOG-0137 | Short-circuit logical or | `cond1 or cond2` | E023 if operand not bool | tested |
| DOG-0138 | Short-circuit logical and | `cond1 and cond2` | E023 if operand not bool | tested |
| DOG-0139 | Logical negation not | `not flag` | E021 if operand not bool | tested |
| DOG-0140 | Addition & text concatenation | `a + b` | number+number, decimal+decimal, text+text | tested |
| DOG-0141 | Subtraction operator | `a - b` | Matching numeric types only | tested |
| DOG-0142 | Multiplication operator | `a * b` | Matching numeric types only | tested |
| DOG-0143 | Division with zero guard | `a / b` | E031 on divide by zero | tested |
| DOG-0144 | Integer modulo operator | `a % b` | number % number only; E031 on % 0 | tested |
| DOG-0145 | Unary numeric negation | `-x` | number or decimal only | tested |
| DOG-0146 | Parenthesized grouping | `(a + b) * c` | Preserves explicit precedence | tested |
| DOG-0147 | Dot field/variant access | `obj.field / Enum.Variant` | E010 on missing field or variant | tested |
| DOG-0148 | Zero-based bracket indexing | `items[i] / txt[i]` | E034 on negative or >= len | tested |
| DOG-0149 | Compound addition & concat assignment | `var_x += expr` | Supports number, decimal, text; E012 on let | tested |
| DOG-0150 | Compound subtract/multiply assignment | `var_x -= expr / *= expr` | Numeric types only; E012 on let | tested |
| DOG-0151 | Compound divide/modulo assignment | `var_x /= expr / %= expr` | Enforces E031 divide-by-zero protection | tested |
| DOG-0152 | Operators & Expressions: Range expressions, bitwise operators, pipe composition, and compound assignment (item 4) | `roadmap::d03::cap_04` | Must uphold strict typing, zero implicit coercion, and explicit E-code diagnostics | specified |

## 5. Collections & Data Structures (70 Capabilities: DOG-0231..DOG-0300)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0231 | Homogeneous list<T> literal | `[a, b, c]` | E021 on mixed element types | tested |
| DOG-0232 | Mutable list push() | `push(var_list, item)` | E012 if list is let/const | tested |
| DOG-0233 | Mutable list pop() | `pop(var_list) -> T` | E034 if list is empty | tested |
| DOG-0234 | Indexed list element update | `var_list[i] = val` | E012 if immutable; E034 on bounds | implemented |
| DOG-0235 | Collection len() inspection | `len(list/map/text) -> number` | Returns element/char count | tested |
| DOG-0236 | String split() into list<text> | `split(text, sep)` | Empty sep splits by UTF-8 chars | tested |
| DOG-0237 | List join() into text | `join(list<text>, sep)` | Concatenates with delimiter | tested |
| DOG-0238 | Typed map<K, V> literal | `{"k": v}` | E021 on mixed key/value types | tested |
| DOG-0239 | Strict map_get() lookup | `map_get(m, key)` | E034 on missing key (no silent null) | tested |
| DOG-0240 | Mutable map_set() insert/update | `map_set(var_m, k, v)` | E012 on immutable map | tested |
| DOG-0241 | Map membership map_has() | `map_has(m, k) -> bool` | Type-checked key lookup | tested |
| DOG-0242 | Map key list map_keys() | `map_keys(m) -> list<K>` | Returns insertion-ordered keys | implemented |
| DOG-0243 | Mutable map_remove() | `map_remove(var_m, k) -> bool` | Returns true if key existed | tested |
| DOG-0244 | Indexed list insertion & removal | `insert_at(var_l, i, v) / remove_at(var_l, i)` | E012 on immutable; E034 on out-of-bounds | tested |
| DOG-0245 | Mutable list clear() | `clear(var_l) -> void` | Empties list in place while keeping type | tested |
| DOG-0246 | List search & sub-range slice | `list_index_of(l, v) / list_slice(l, s, e)` | Polymorphic equality & safe bounds slicing | tested |
| DOG-0247 | Map value extraction map_values() | `map_values(m) -> list<V>` | Preserves insertion order matching map_keys() | tested |
| DOG-0248 | Mutable map_clear() & map_merge() | `map_clear(var_m) / map_merge(var_dst, src)` | In-place mutation with key/value type checking | tested |

| DOG-0301 | Struct definition | `struct Name field: type end` | E004 on empty struct; E011 on dup field | tested |
| DOG-0302 | Complete struct initialization | `Name { field: val }` | E004 if any field omitted | tested |
| DOG-0303 | Struct field reading | `instance.field` | E010 on unknown field | tested |
| DOG-0304 | Mutable struct field update | `var_instance.field = val` | E012 if binding is let/const | tested |
| DOG-0305 | Enum definition | `enum Name Variant ... end` | Explicit variant set | tested |
| DOG-0306 | Enum variant construction | `Name.Variant` | E010 on undefined variant | tested |
| DOG-0307 | Enum equality & match | `case Name.Variant` | Exhaustive type-checked matching | tested |
| DOG-0308 | Struct/Enum JSON serialization | `json_stringify(struct_val)` | Serializes struct fields cleanly | implemented |

## 8. Error Handling (40 Capabilities: DOG-0431..DOG-0470)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0431 | Structured try/catch/end blocks | `try ... catch err ... end` | Binds caught error title as text | tested |
| DOG-0432 | Explicit recoverable fail() | `fail(reason_text)` | Raises catchable E035 diagnostic | tested |
| DOG-0433 | Runtime assert() contract | `assert(cond, msg?)` | Raises E033 diagnostic on false | tested |
| DOG-0434 | 4-part diagnostic layout | `What / WHY / FIX / TIP+DOG` | Preserves technical clarity across modes | tested |
| DOG-0435 | Exact source span pointers | `file:line:column + caret ^` | Points directly to offending token | tested |
| DOG-0436 | Personality diagnostic modes | `normal / sarcastic / chaotic / professional / silent` | Configurable via `dog config personality` | implemented |
| DOG-0437 | Exhaustive try/catch return check | `Both try and catch return` | Verified statically in TypeChecker | implemented |
| DOG-0438 | Levenshtein "Did you mean?" hints | `E004 / E010 / E013 / CLI typo suggestions` | Suggests closest in-scope var, fn, field, type, or CLI command | tested |

## 10. Networking & APIs (70 Capabilities: DOG-0531..DOG-0600)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0531 | Plain HTTP GET client | `http_get(url) -> text` | 10s connect/read/write timeouts; explains HTTPS TLS | implemented |
| DOG-0532 | Local static HTTP server | `dog server [dir] [port]` | Directory listings, MIME types, traversal block | implemented |
| DOG-0533 | Network timeout diagnostics | `E040 on DNS/connect/read error` | Catchable inside try/catch | implemented |
| DOG-0534 | Local HTTP server & static asset reply | `http_listen() / http_accept() / http_serve_file()` | Ephemeral :0 binding, MIME types, path traversal guard | tested |
| DOG-0535 | Non-blocking HTTP poll accept | `http_try_accept(srv, timeout_ms)` | Returns map with _ok="true" or "false" cleanly | tested |
| DOG-0536 | Chunked HTTP request body decoding | `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` | Decodes chunk sizes up to 8 MiB on server & client | tested |
| DOG-0537 | HTTP redirects & custom response headers | `http_reply_headers() / http_redirect()` | Supports arbitrary headers & 301/302/303/307/308 | tested |
| DOG-0538 | Redirect-following HTTP/HTTPS client | `http_request() / http_get() / http_post() / http_put() / http_delete()` | Follows up to 5 hops; returns status, body, headers, final_url, ok | tested |
| DOG-0539 | Custom-timeout HTTP request API | `http_request_timeout(m, url, h, body, ms)` | Configurable per-call connect/read timeout | tested |
| DOG-0540 | Pattern-matching HTTP router | `http_route_match("/users/:id/*rest", path)` | Extracts named params + _matched="true" or "false" | tested |
| DOG-0541 | Structured URL & query string codecs | `url_parse() / query_parse() / query_stringify()` | Parses scheme, host, port, path, query, IPv6 authorities | tested |
| DOG-0542 | RFC 6455 WebSocket server & client | `ws_upgrade() / ws_connect() / ws_send() / ws_recv() / ws_close()` | SHA-1 Sec-WebSocket-Accept handshake, masking, Ping/Pong, Close | tested |
| DOG-0543 | Raw TCP sockets with timeout & peer info | `tcp_listen() / tcp_connect() / tcp_set_timeout() / tcp_remote_addr()` | Stateful TCP handle table + E040 error mapping | tested |
| DOG-0544 | Native UDP datagram networking | `udp_bind() / udp_send_to() / udp_recv_from() / udp_close()` | Unconnected datagrams returning data & sender from addr | tested |
| DOG-0545 | DNS host lookup & TCP port probe | `dns_lookup(host) / port_is_open(addr, ms)` | Resolves IPv4/IPv6 addresses & non-blocking reachability check | tested |

## 11. File & System Operations (60 Capabilities: DOG-0601..DOG-0660)

| ID | Capability | Syntax / API | Edge Cases & Contracts | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-0601 | Read UTF-8 text file | `file_read(path) -> text` | E040 on missing/unreadable file | implemented |
| DOG-0602 | Write UTF-8 text file | `file_write(path, content)` | E040 on permission/path error | implemented |
| DOG-0603 | Check file existence | `file_exists(path) -> bool` | Safe non-throwing check | implemented |
| DOG-0604 | Delete file safely | `file_delete(path) -> bool` | Returns false if missing | implemented |
| DOG-0605 | Sorted directory listing | `dir_list(path) -> list<text>` | E040 if path unreadable | implemented |
| DOG-0606 | Environment variable lookup | `env_get(key) -> text` | Returns empty string if unset | implemented |
| DOG-0607 | Environment variable mutation | `env_set(key, val)` | Updates current process env | implemented |
| DOG-0608 | Host & Termux doctor probe | `dog doctor` | Detects OS/Arch, Termux PREFIX, rustc, TTY | implemented |
| DOG-0609 | System & process telemetry CLI | `dog system / process / permissions` | Reads /proc/self/status RSS & UID | implemented |
| DOG-0610 | Masked environment dump | `dog env` | Redacts KEY/TOKEN/SECRET/PASS/AUTH vars | implemented |
| DOG-0611 | File copy, size & metadata probes | `file_copy() / file_size() / is_file() / is_dir()` | Clean E040 errors on permission/path failure | tested |
| DOG-0612 | Cross-platform path joining | `path_join(a, b) -> text` | Normalizes separators cleanly across POSIX/Windows | tested |
| DOG-0613 | ISO-8601 UTC timestamp helper | `time_utc() -> text` | Pure-Rust Hinnant civil calendar conversion | tested |

| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| DOG-1041 | Mobile soft word-wrap engine | `Alt+W / :wrap (auto ON when cols < 72)` | Wraps long logical lines cleanly onto visual rows without clipping | tested |
| DOG-1042 | Continuation gutter marker | `build_visual_rows() '  ↳  ' gutter` | Preserves 5-col alignment across wrapped continuations | tested |
| DOG-1043 | Width-bounded mobile header bar | `render_editor_frame_rows() header budget` | Guarantees header never wraps on 36-col phone terminals | tested |
| DOG-1044 | Scrollable output & help overlay | `Up/Down/PgUp/PgDn/Home scroll state` | Wraps and paginates long stdout/error overlays within screen width | tested |
| DOG-1045 | ANSI-aware visual width counter | `visible_ansi_len(s) -> usize` | Ignores CSI escape bytes when clamping frame rows to terminal cols | tested |
| DOG-1046 | Tab-stop visual expansion | `visual_expand() & logical_to_visual_col()` | Maps cursor columns accurately across tab-indented lines | tested |
| DOG-1047 | Scrolled prompt input tail | `scroll_prompt_tail() in find/palette/open` | Keeps interactive prompts single-line on narrow Termux viewports | tested |
| DOG-1048 | Robust Termux size probing | `query_terminal_size() env + stty + tput` | Ignores 0x0 stty responses and clamps safely to >= 24x8 | tested |
| DOG-1049 | Narrow popup suggestion layout | `render_suggestion_popup_overlay() >= 22 cols` | Shows IntelliSense completion list cleanly even on 36-col phones | tested |

| DOG-1241 | Single-char UTF-8 lookup | `char_at(s: text, idx: number) -> text` | Bounds-checked Unicode scalar lookup (E033 on out-of-range) | tested |
| DOG-1242 | Reverse substring search | `last_index_of(haystack, needle) -> number` | Returns char index of rightmost match or -1 | tested |
| DOG-1243 | Non-overlapping occurrence counter | `count_matches(haystack, needle) -> number` | Returns 0 for empty needle and counts exact matches | tested |
| DOG-1244 | Safe prefix & suffix strippers | `remove_prefix(s, p) / remove_suffix(s, f)` | Returns original text unchanged when fix is absent | tested |
| DOG-1245 | Word title-casing & reversal | `title_case(s) / reverse_text(s) / lines(s)` | Unicode-aware word casing, char reversal, and line splitting | tested |
| DOG-1246 | Deduplicated & sliced lists | `list_unique / list_take / list_skip` | Preserves insertion order while slicing non-destructively | tested |
| DOG-1247 | List flattening & reductions | `list_flatten / list_concat / list_sum / list_avg` | Strict element-typed list concatenation and numeric aggregation | tested |
| DOG-1248 | Zip map builder & default lookup | `map_from_lists(k, v) / map_get_or(m, k, fb)` | Builds deduplicated maps and avoids E034 on optional keys | tested |
